# The values-checks of the LOCK-m-F0 v1.0 draft (results/raw/2026-10-15_m-lock-v1.0-draft_pre-gpwr.md), of the
# re-anchoring raw's dispositions and generator, and of the RESULTS entry that commits them with G-PWR's program
# (PROPOSAL-m v1.11 §8 step 15). Code stamp: 2026-10-15 20:35 UTC (date -u). Read-only checks; no agent opened data/m/ckpt/* or
# data/m/reports/*; no stage duration computed or quoted.
# Passes on the draft: 1, two lenses (facts; fidelity to the rulings), each with a strict refuter; 2, the same two lenses
#   afresh, each first checking that pass 1's dispositions landed, each with a strict refuter; 3, a check of every line
#   pass 2 changed, with a strict refuter. Then the draft's changes after pass 3 (diffs, split by which check saw
#   them), and the entry: two passes, each with a strict refuter, which also check those changes and every filled
#   value; a third that checks pass 2's changes (to the entry, the draft, the review raw's header, the dispositions
#   and the tools), and a fourth that checks pass 3's, each with a strict refuter. Each pass: the
#   workflow's script, every return verbatim, and Code's dispositions. The draft's placeholders (@@…@@) are filled
#   mechanically at the snapshot from the committed bytes (fill_snapshot.py, verbatim at the end).

====================================================================================================
THE DRAFT, PASS 1
====================================================================================================

---- THE WORKFLOW SCRIPT, verbatim
export const meta = {
  name: 'v10-draft-vc1',
  description: 'Values-check pass 1 of the LOCK v1.0 draft (pre-G-PWR snapshot) and the re-anchoring dispositions: facts; fidelity to the rulings; each with a strict refuter',
  phases: [{ title: 'Check' }, { title: 'Refute' }],
}
const R = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const L = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/lockv1'
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. Values-check PASS 1 of texts Code will commit (repo ${R}, HEAD 2186659): (A) the LOCK-m-F0 v1.0 draft amendment ${L}/v10_amend.md, to be committed as results/raw/2026-10-15_m-lock-v1.0-draft_pre-gpwr.md (a snapshot naming the analyzer before G-PWR runs; [PENDING] items are deliberate); the full file it assembles into is ${L}/LOCK-m-F0.v1.0-draft.md (by ${L}/assemble_v10.py: the amendment, then the v0.2 blob at db7d970 with four truncations); (B) Code's re-anchoring dispositions ${L}/disp_reanchor.md, carried in ${L}/reanchoring_raw.txt with the map and refuter verbatim (${L}/reanchor.json); (C) the PIN-index addendum ${L}/pin_index_addendum_v10.txt (generator ${L}/gen_addendum_v10.py), which v1.0 §8 describes. Sources: the current LOCK ${R}/LOCK-m-F0.md (v0.2 + v0.1 text); PROPOSAL-m ${R}/PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md (m:N); the seat's step-15 answers ${R}/results/raw/2026-10-14_m-design-to-code_step15_answers.md; the step-14 read ${R}/results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-F0m-Qwen_read.md; the step-10 read ${R}/results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-TIME-F0_read.md; Code's step-15 and step-15b pings and the addendum in ${R}/results/raw/; RESULTS.md; the preprints ${R}/PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md (v8) and ${R}/PREPRINT-v1_1-draft.md; git history (git log, git show; read-only). The PI's words of 2026-10-14 are in RESULTS.md's step-15b entry; of 2026-10-15 in the addendum.
HARD RULES. Read-only; no git writes; read nothing under data/m/ckpt/ or data/m/reports/; never open results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt; no git in jlens-qwen36/; write only under ${L}/vc1/; do not read scripts/audit_note_*, results/raw/*note-*, README-NOTE.md, RESEARCH-NOTE-*, AUDIT-note-*, LICENS*, root *.svg, ADDENDUM-k1.md.`
const SCHEMA = { type: 'object', properties: {
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, where: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['error', 'imprecise', 'note'] },
    finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'where', 'severity', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  verified: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['findings', 'verified'] }
const VER = { type: 'object', properties: {
  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
const LENSES = [
  { key: 'facts', task: `Check EVERY factual statement in (A), (B) and (C) against its source: each sha256 (recompute) and commit (git log), each quotation byte-exact (the PI's, the seat's, m:N), each line citation, each count and list (e.g. the truncations: are the four the only full occurrences of superseded tested hashes? the PIN-index sites; the re-anchoring's five no-counterpart notes and the section translation against the two preprints), each file path. Run the assembler and check its claims (v0.2 tail byte-exact apart from four truncations; the superseded analyzer nowhere in full).` },
  { key: 'fidelity', task: `Check (A) against the rulings it implements, as the seat would: does it carry every item m:286, the step-14 read §4 item 5, the step-10 read's Q1/Q2/Q5, R2 3, R1 C4/C6/C7, the answers' Q1/Q3/Q4/Q7 and "The not-questions", and the step-15b ping's Code defaults (marked pending the seat) require, in the form they require (e.g. the truncation rule, "kept in full" items, the PIN-index addendum's form, the Q5 sentence, the strike-window sentence)? Is anything stated as ruled that is Code's, or Code's that is ruled? Is anything required missing, or anything added without being marked as Code's? Does (B) apply the re-anchoring refuter's verdicts faithfully? Is v1.0 §2's re-anchoring summary faithful to (B)?` },
]
const res = await pipeline(LENSES,
  l => agent(COMMON + `\n\nTASK (${l.key}): ${l.task} Work dir ${L}/vc1/${l.key}.`, { label: 'v10vc1:' + l.key, phase: 'Check', schema: SCHEMA }),
  (rep, l) => rep ? agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER of this values-check report (${l.key}), verbatim:\n${JSON.stringify(rep)}\nFor EACH finding reproduce it and give CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with evidence and the exact fix; default to REFUTED if you cannot reproduce. Re-verify 3 'verified' items. Add to 'missed' anything wrong in (A), (B) or (C) the report did not find. Work dir ${L}/vc1/ref_${l.key}.`, { label: 'v10vc1-ref:' + l.key, phase: 'Refute', schema: VER }).then(v => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: v })) : null)
return res

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 1 (disp_vc1.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of the LOCK v1.0 draft's values-check, pass 1 (two lenses, each with a strict refuter)

Applied in the draft amendment (v10_amend.md), the re-anchoring dispositions (disp_reanchor.md) and the re-anchoring
raw's generator, in the refuter's form where the verdict was PARTLY. Several fixes landed while the refuters ran; the
refuters checked both the reviewed and the current texts.

## Fidelity (FID-1–FID-18)

- **FID-1 (PIN-45's limits):** the three limits as PIN-45's text states them ("No V2 refusal catches any of the
  three"), and, separately, the two checks the answers' Q2 declares not built.
- **FID-2:** a sixth dated "no counterpart" note: the Fallback-1 title phrase at v1.1:3 against v8:3's Main title.
- **FID-3:** the map's flags carried to v1.0 §2 as "flagged, not adjudicated", with the step-15b ping's Q2 label
  default marked [PENDING the seat], and the dose-note flag added to the dispositions.
- **FID-4:** "sha256 and commits in v1.0 §14".
- **FID-5:** the classification rules marked as Code's readings, strikable until v1.0's commit (the ping's Q1 default,
  kept by the addendum except for the hold) [PENDING the seat].
- **FID-6:** the first round's index changes on Q1's terms; the second round's changed blocks on the same terms, Code's
  additions in them put in the ping's Q4 [PENDING the seat]; P301's only change is Q5's ordered correction (the facts
  refuter's miss).
- **FID-7 (PARTLY, the refuter's form):** the register stays "Code's readings, none struck", with R2 2's exception as
  lock §9.3 states it; a table records the seat's step-15 words against the entries they touched (PIN-44–46, PIN-45,
  PIN-17, PIN-7/9/10); Code's additions are the ping's Q4 list. The "made its own" wording of an intermediate draft is
  withdrawn.
- **FID-8:** the cap's inputs cite the step-14 read's §4 item 2 beside v0.2 §2 (its words, without restating its
  figures).
- **FID-9:** G-PWR's draft check is Code's mechanism for R2 3's order, and is in §0's list of Code's items.
- **FID-10:** the class relabels, the superseded step-15 entry reading, and lock §9.3's PIN-17 row restated in the
  placed copy's coordinates (Code's restatement on the corrected anchor).
- **FID-11, FID-13, FID-14 (the dispositions):** the clause-(b) paraphrase sites; #47 kept as m's own with its evidence
  corrected and #94 given "root coordinate, replaced"; the CONFIRMED verdicts that carry fixes (#5, #9/#41, #37) listed.
- **FID-12:** the licence window runs from `b046b16`.
- **FID-15:** the hold is the PI's choice of Code's option; its description is Code's words.
- **FID-16:** the placeholder rows name the superseded manifests where the full value stands.
- **FID-17 (PARTLY):** (i) the placement inside step 15, against m:58's and m:356's "before step 15" and m:58's "redraft
  from v1.1", recorded with Code's reading that the PI's "placed" settles both, marked [PENDING the PI] and listed among
  Code's items; (ii) the decisions block's records of (6′) and (7) quoted verbatim with m:16's marks, as step 3 did
  (m:356).
- **FID-18 (PARTLY):** note 5 names m:349's "§7.1's title sentences"; note 2 names m:291's and m:361's Step-6 text (and
  the dispositions' m:358/m:361 bullet adds m:361's); notes 1, 3 and 4 also stand as the kinds' re-anchoring pending
  Q5; Q7's planning row is among G-PWR's inputs.
- **The fidelity refuter's misses:** §14's addendum row filled (and a row for the interim raw); the sixth Q4 referent
  (§7.5's seed items) pointed to notes 3–4 and the kinds; the ping's Q3 default's second half ("replaced in place as the
  builder's output, not moved by PIN-37"); two of the map's flags carried (§7.5's first sentence; clause (b) reaching
  :3, :13, :230, :234); anchors at `2186659`.

## Facts (F1–F8)

- **F1 (PARTLY):** P315, P317 and P318 each truncated at a new v1.0 marker (:550, :736, :819), P317's `wanted` line also
  rewritten.
- **F2:** the refuter's "#N" is the 0-based index into the mapper's own map.json (100 of 100 labels match it; 57 match
  the workflow's return order). The raw now prints map.json's referents numbered so, beside the return. An intermediate
  header that said otherwise is corrected (the facts refuter's miss).
- **F3–F8:** the §14 rows; the lock-text readers named (the driver's precheck, PIN-40); the inner-quote convention, with
  lock §0's and v0.2 §0's conventions adopted explicitly; the superseded first-round analyzer written to 16 hex; the
  sixth Q4 referent; "at root:248's coordinate" and "the abstract clause verbatim at v1.1:13 and v8:13".
- **The facts refuter's misses:** the step-14 read's quote marks its dropped bold; the v1.1 reference's sha256 is noted
  as bound by no check; "the references run to the header note" in lowercase.

CONTEXT. Sources: the current LOCK ${R}/LOCK-m-F0.md (v0.2 + v0.1 text); PROPOSAL-m ${R}/PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md (m:N); the seat's step-15 answers ${R}/results/raw/2026-10-14_m-design-to-code_step15_answers.md; the step-14 read ${R}/results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-F0m-Qwen_read.md; the step-10 read ${R}/results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-TIME-F0_read.md; Code's step-15 and step-15b pings and the step-15b addendum in ${R}/results/raw/; RESULTS.md; the preprints ${R}/PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md (v8) and ${R}/PREPRINT-v1_1-draft.md; git history (read-only). HARD RULES. Read-only; no git writes; read nothing under data/m/ckpt/ or data/m/reports/; never open results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt; no git in jlens-qwen36/; write only under ${L}/vc2/; do not read scripts/audit_note_*, results/raw/*note-*, README-NOTE.md, RESEARCH-NOTE-*, AUDIT-note-*, LICENS*, root *.svg, ADDENDUM-k1.md.`
const SCHEMA = { type: 'object', properties: {
  pass1_applied: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' }, applied: { type: 'string', enum: ['YES', 'NO', 'PARTLY'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'applied', 'evidence'] } },
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, where: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['error', 'imprecise', 'note'] },
    finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'where', 'severity', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  verified: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['pass1_applied', 'findings', 'verified'] }
const VER = { type: 'object', properties: {
  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
const LENSES = [
  { key: 'facts', task: `First, for each pass-1 facts finding and refuter miss in ${L}/vc1.json, check that Code's disposition in ${L}/disp_vc1.md landed in (A)-(C) as stated (pass1_applied). Then check EVERY factual statement in (A), (B) and (C) afresh against its source, as pass 1's facts lens did but without relying on its 'verified' list: each sha256 (recompute) and commit (git log), each quotation byte-exact, each line citation (m:N against the proposal; v8:N and v1.1:N against the preprints), each count and list, each file path, each date. Run the assembler and the generators and check their claims.` },
  { key: 'fidelity', task: `First, for each pass-1 fidelity finding and refuter miss in ${L}/vc1.json, check that Code's disposition in ${L}/disp_vc1.md landed as stated (pass1_applied). Then check (A) afresh against the rulings it implements, as the seat would: m:286, m:58, m:356, m:371, the step-14 read's §4 items, the step-10 read's Q1/Q2/Q5/Q6, R2 3, R1 C4/C6/C7, the answers' Q1-Q7 and "The not-questions" and "The order from here", the PI's words (PI 1-4 of 2026-10-14, "placed", the hold of 2026-10-15). Is anything stated as ruled that is Code's, or Code's that is ruled? Is any [PENDING] marker missing where a value or ruling is still open, or present where it is settled? Is the order of the remaining steps (G-PWR, the cap, the table's hash after the seat's Q2, the commit by 2026-10-30) stated as the PI's hold set it? Is anything required missing, or added without being marked as Code's?` },
]
const res = await pipeline(LENSES,
  l => agent(COMMON + `\n\nTASK (${l.key}): ${l.task} Work dir ${L}/vc2/${l.key}.`, { label: 'v10vc2:' + l.key, phase: 'Check', schema: SCHEMA }),
  (rep, l) => rep ? agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER of this values-check report (${l.key}, pass 2), verbatim:\n${JSON.stringify(rep)}\nFor EACH finding and each pass1_applied entry that is not YES, reproduce it and give CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with evidence and the exact fix; default to REFUTED if you cannot reproduce. Re-verify 3 'verified' items and 3 pass1_applied YES entries. Add to 'missed' anything wrong in (A), (B) or (C) the report did not find. Work dir ${L}/vc2/ref_${l.key}.`, { label: 'v10vc2-ref:' + l.key, phase: 'Refute', schema: VER }).then(v => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: v })) : null)
return res

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 2 (disp_vc2.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of the LOCK v1.0 draft's values-check, pass 2 (two lenses, each with a strict refuter)

Pass 2 checked that pass 1's dispositions (disp_vc1.md) landed, then checked the draft (A), the re-anchoring raw and
its dispositions (B) and the PIN-index addendum (C) afresh. Every finding is applied in the refuter's form unless
stated, in the draft amendment (v10_amend.md), the re-anchoring dispositions (disp_reanchor.md) and the raw's
generator (gen_reanchor_raw.py). (C) needed no change. The regenerated raw is (B) as committed.

## Pass 1, as landed

- **F1 (PARTLY) and N9:** the marker sites are now in the text. v1.0 §8 gives P315 at P324 (`:550`), P317 at P325
  (`:736`) and P318 at P326 (`:819`).

  quotation conventions (N5).

- The other pass-1 items landed as disp_vc1.md states. The refuter re-verified F2, F3 and F5's first half, FID-1,
  FID-16 and refuter-miss 8.

  and `:557`.

  §9.1 and §10 name theirs. v1.0 §2 also names PIN-40's two hashes in full, beside v1.0 §6. A quotation keeps the hashes
  it quotes. §0's pointer to §2 stays true.

  dispositions' opening sentence now says so.

CONTEXT. Pass 2 of the values-check of the LOCK-m-F0 v1.0 draft (${L}/vc2.json) produced findings that Code applied; Code's dispositions are ${L}/disp_vc2.md. The changes are in ${L}/vc3/delta.diff (v10_amend.pre-vc2.md -> v10_amend.md; disp_reanchor.pre-vc2.md -> disp_reanchor.md; gen_reanchor_raw.pre-vc2.py -> gen_reanchor_raw.py). The draft is ${L}/v10_amend.md (placeholders @@PROGRAM@@, @@PINIDX@@, @@PINIDX16@@, @@REANCHOR@@, @@PROGRAM16@@, @@REVIEW@@ are filled mechanically at the snapshot from committed bytes and are deliberate; [PENDING] items are deliberate). The regenerated raw is ${L}/reanchoring_raw.txt. Sources (repo ${R}, HEAD 2186659): LOCK-m-F0.md; PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md (m:N); results/raw/2026-10-14_m-design-to-code_step15_answers.md; results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-F0m-Qwen_read.md; results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-TIME-F0_read.md; results/raw/2026-10-15_m-code-to-design_step15b.md and its addendum; RESULTS.md; scripts/m_sessions.py; scripts/m_analyze.py; git history (read-only).
HARD RULES. Read-only; no git writes; read nothing under data/m/ckpt/ or data/m/reports/; never open results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt; no git in jlens-qwen36/; write only under ${L}/vc3/; do not read scripts/audit_note_*, results/raw/*note-*, README-NOTE.md, RESEARCH-NOTE-*, AUDIT-note-*, LICENS*, root *.svg, ADDENDUM-k1.md.`
const SCHEMA = { type: 'object', properties: {
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, where: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['error', 'imprecise', 'note'] },
    finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'where', 'severity', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  verified: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['findings', 'verified'] }
const VER = { type: 'object', properties: {
  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
phase('Check')
const rep = await agent(COMMON + `\n\nTASK. Check EVERY added or changed line in the delta against its source: each quotation byte-exact (after the declared conventions: wrapped lines joined with one space, inner double quotes as single quotes, bold dropped where said), each line or site citation (m_sessions.py lines; P-site numbers against ${L}/pin_index_addendum_v10.txt and lock §10), each commit and its description (git log), each sha256 (recompute), each attribution (is anything stated as the seat's or the PI's that is Code's, or vice versa?), each [PENDING] marker (present where open, absent where settled). Check that disp_vc2.md describes what the delta does, no more and no less, and that each change answers the pass-2 finding it cites in ${L}/vc2.json. Check the regenerated raw's header against its contents. Work dir ${L}/vc3/check.`, { label: 'v10vc3:check', phase: 'Check', schema: SCHEMA })
phase('Refute')
const ref = await agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER of this values-check report, verbatim:\n${JSON.stringify(rep)}\nFor EACH finding reproduce it and give CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with evidence and the exact fix; default to REFUTED if you cannot reproduce. Re-verify 3 'verified' items. Add to 'missed' anything wrong in the delta or disp_vc2.md the report did not find. Work dir ${L}/vc3/ref.`, { label: 'v10vc3:refute', phase: 'Refute', schema: VER })
return { report: rep, refute: ref }

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 3 (disp_vc3.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of the delta check of pass 2's changes (one checker, with a strict refuter)

The delta check covered every line pass 2 changed in the draft (v10_amend.md), the re-anchoring dispositions and the
raw's generator, and checked disp_vc2.md against them. It raised eleven findings. The refuter confirmed seven and
confirmed four in part (VC3-2, VC3-3, VC3-9, VC3-11), and it added six misses. All are applied in the refuter's form,
and disp_vc2.md is corrected where it said more or less than the text.

- **VC3-1:** the raw's header now limits its quotation conventions to Code's own text, the header and the
  dispositions. The workflow's script, the map, the map.json listing and the refuter are printed as written. The raw
  is regenerated.
- **VC3-2 (PARTLY):** the three remaining parts of pass-2 refuter fixes are applied:
  - (a) v1.0 §8's analyzer bullet repeats the terms and R2 2's exception;
  - (b) v1.0 §3 points to v1.0 §1's "What would move these lines";
  - (c) v1.0 §5 says the seat's Q2 ruling is on its path, in the addendum's words ("The ruling is now on that path").

  disp_vc2.md now describes the header as it stands.
- **VC3-3 (PARTLY):** v1.0 §3 names the command of record. It reads the committed snapshot, and its raw's header
  states it with the launch check before it. disp_vc2.md's sentence now points to it.
- **VC3-4 and the miss on Code's step-15 ping:** the header takes [PENDING the PI] out of the default rule. Such an item
  waits on the PI's word, which Code asks for with the cap, since Code's step-15 ping calls the point "the PI's to
  say". The header also names "its run" as a way an item is settled.
- **VC3-5:** a Q4 strike's redo is stated on the ping's Q4 terms: the analyzer, and with the kind's name the
  placeholder and the table's kinds. The preprint is not part of it.
- **VC3-6:** the table's steps after the ruling cover (C), which rebuilds from the new copy, and the values-check of the
  sweep under (A4).
- **VC3-7:** v1.0 §0's rulings add the step-14 read's §4 item 3 and "What Code does now" 3. R2 2's line names the index
  sites, the stage-start check's half among them.
- **VC3-8:** v1.0 §0's list of Code's items adds the three readings marked in §§1–2.
- **VC3-9 (PARTLY):** two changes to v1.0 §14:
  - the "with this draft's snapshot" cells carry **[PENDING: its commit, named at v1.0]**;
  - a row for the snapshot itself, whose sha256 G-PWR's record carries.
- **VC3-10:** the four optional notes have their own heading in disp_reanchor.md, "Optional notes, not taken".
- **VC3-11 (PARTLY):** v1.0 §0's identity-build line and v1.0 §3's opening bullet are rewrapped. No value changes.
- **Misses:**
  - Under (C), v1.0 §2's v8-specific parts are redone against v9: the preprint's hash, PIN-17's re-verification and
    the re-anchoring.
  - A Q7 strike recomputes G-PWR's figures only.
  - The header says that in the snapshot nothing follows the amendment.
  - The §14 row for the PIN-index addendum gains its path.
  - The generator's docstring names the map.json listing.
  - The refuter's re-verifications need no change.

const SCHEMA = { type: 'object', properties: {
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, where: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['error', 'imprecise', 'note'] },

  verified: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['findings', 'verified'] }
const VER = { type: 'object', properties: {
  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
const TASK1 = `TASK (pass 1). Check EVERY factual statement in the entry against its source: each sha256 (recompute from the repo files), each count (rounds, builds, wrapper/verifier versions, probes 56, spot-checks 11, the 214 and 96 mutant batches, the self-test), each defect and the round it is attributed to (against the round results and Code's dispositions), each claim about what the program, the wrapper and the verifier do (against their code), each path, the figures sha256 and 'unchanged from r1 to r13' (against figs_*.out and the dispositions), each quotation, and the 'Next' list against the plan in the dispositions. ---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 1 (disp_entry_vc1.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of the entry's values-check, pass 1 (one checker, with a strict refuter)

The refuter confirmed 16 of the 18 findings and confirmed I9 and N17 in part. It added six misses. All are applied in
the refuter's form, and the raws and the snapshot are regenerated. The snapshot's sha256 and the review raw's change;
the entry names the new values.

## Errors

- **E1:** the command of record gains `-X utf8`, in the draft's v1.0 §3 (with "the jlens-qwen36 interpreter, in a
  scrubbed environment") and in the entry's "Next" item 2.
- **E2:** round 3's two build diffs (r3 → r4, r4 → r5) were appended inside round 7's block, a generator defect. They
  are back in round 3's section.
- **E3:** round 7's attack had 20 routes: 14 refused, 4 canonical, 2 OTHER. The tally is now quoted exactly.
- **E4:** disp_r13's claim is replaced. P2u wrote a wrong record past the in-process figures pin, which hashes the dict
  and not the text written. Against committed code the out-of-process verifier is the binding.

## Imprecisions and notes

- **I5:** disp_r12's v1 trials. The lawful trial's output was not kept; only the tamper trial's raw is in the review
  raw.
- **I6 and the misses on V6 and the fsck warning:** disp_r14's replay summary is split by outcome: those v1 passed,
  V6's adapted form still passing v2, V8 and V7b now stated, the duplicates not run on v1, P2u failing on both, and
  the fsck warning.
- **I7 and the miss on r1/r2:** the figures claim now names the dry runs that exist (r1, r2, r3, r5) and r2's dropped
  lines, in the entry and in the raw's header.
- **I8:** the second verifier "tightens" each check. The entry says what round 10 found still loose, and that the third
  makes it exact.
- **I9 (PARTLY):** the battery's run on the committed blob gets its own raw, `results/raw/2026-10-15_m-gpwr-probes.txt`,
  committed with the run's raw, because the run's raw is bound byte for byte.
- **I10, I11:** the draft's "Around the run" bullet names the closure and the byte-for-byte binding. The §6 closure row
  lists its 13 files and says each value is the file's sha256.
- **I12:** the entry lists the [PENDING] items as "include", with the three it had left out.
- **N13:** the values-check raw is generated and committed. The entry says the draft's last changes and the fills are
  checked in this entry's two passes.
- **N14:** a stamp line, filled at the commit.
- **N15:** the comparison section is titled "r9 THROUGH r13". The raw's header names round 1's mutation list.
- **N16:** disp_r14 records the refuter's disclosure as given, the leftover empty directories (the refuter's 21 and
  Code's two, for the user to remove), and V8 on a lawful tree as accepted fail-closed behaviour.
- **N17 (PARTLY, the refuter's form):** disp_r11 says why scripts/ needs no tree scan: it is not on the import path
  under -I, r12's finder makes other repository modules absent, and the script and the analyzer are bound by blob.
- **N18:** four rewordings:
  - (a) the concurrent swap is "in the attack's run" (entry and disp_r9);
  - (b) "bound out of process, with r13, by the wrapper's fsck and the verifier";
  - (c) round 9's attribution to the attack's reading, confirmed by the refuter's runs, and the refuter's own find;
  - (d, e) "the sha256 of every bound file (the package by its formula)" and "every other repository module loaded".
- **The miss on stale "the blob committed":** the raw's header now says each disposition reads as of its round, and
  that r13 is the blob committed.
- **The miss on the mis-ordered tally** (KILLED 51, EQUIVALENT 9, SURVIVED 36 on r6) concerns the report's own
  "verified" item. No committed text is affected.
- **The miss on line anchors:** noted.
- **disp_r12's "whatever the run's process did"** is qualified as within the declared environment, with rounds 9 and
  10's finds. This was found while applying E4.

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 2 (disp_entry_vc2.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of the entry's values-check, pass 2 (one checker, with a strict refuter)

This file reads as of pass 2, except where a line says what a later pass changed.

**Pass 1 landed.** The checker found every pass-1 disposition applied. The refuter re-verified E1, E3, E4, N15 and I7,
gave I7 in part because its wording still was not exact (below), and re-checked three of the checker's verified items
(the shas, the fill, the tools).

**Pass 2 raised 13 findings.** The refuter confirmed ten, confirmed three in part (P2-3, P2-6, P2-8), and added five
misses. All are applied, in the refuter's form except:
- **P2-3:** the restatement clause was dropped, and "the analyzer moves" was dropped at pass 3 on the checker's P3-12(b);
  pass 3 re-cited the date;
- **P2-4:** the interpreter part was applied, the pinned copy declined, and the re-verification met in another form
  (disp_r14);
- **P2-10:** "and pins" was missing from the entry until pass 3;
- **P2-13:** completed at pass 3.

The changes go to a third pass.

## Applied

- **P2-1, when the preparation began (an error).** The seat's answers, "The not-questions", say G-PWR's scratch
  preparation is "on synthetic inputs, uncommitted" and that "its review raw says when it began". The raw did not
  say it. Its header now gives the order, from the scratch files' records, with the refuter's corrections:
  - 2026-10-13: a read-only planning map (R1 A4), which recomputed the planning row on Q-F0's inputs;
  - 2026-10-15: the PI's hold word; then the program's first build; then its first dry run, which computed every
    G-PWR figure on the inputs of record, the sequential false-pause and guard figures new among them; then the v1.0
    draft naming the analyzer.

  Code does not adjudicate this. It is **declared as a departure for the seat**: the dry runs and the rounds used the
  inputs of record. Code's reading is that R2 3's "before G-PWR … runs" governs the run of record. The entry and the
  raw declare it, and the draft's §3 bullet now does too (pass 3); pass 4 made the three statements one form.
- **P2-2, the step-14 read's Q4.** The program's draft check and its verifier read the draft's text and were built
  before its snapshot's commit, although Code's step-15 ping had said the preparation "reads no lock text". This is
  declared in the raw's header, and in the draft and the entry. Code's reading is that a draft snapshot
  under `results/raw/` is not a lock's committed text, and that the run of record follows its commit.
- **P2-3 (PARTLY):** the commit conditions are attributed to their sources:
  - R2 3 and the step-14 read §4 item 5 for G-PWR's run and the cap;
  - the hold, m:286 and PIN-40 for the Q2 ruling;
  - the answers' item 6 and D1 for the date.

  The Q4-strike consequence is marked as Code's reading.
- **P2-4, the interpreter.** The interpreter part of the refuter's fix is applied: the verifier runs under
  `/usr/bin/python3 -I -S -X utf8` (Xcode's, root-owned, Python 3.9). With -S no site module runs, so no .pth file
  reaches it; it imports only the standard library, so it needs none. disp_r14 records the parts. The entry's gap
  now says the program's interpreter and the jlens-qwen36 environment are user-writable, the latter inside the
  repository's directory.
- **P2-5:** HEAD's checks are worded as they are made: at the load, at provenance and before the write, and out of
  process at launch and after the run.
- **P2-6 (PARTLY):** the raw is bound "as it stands at its call". What the wrapper appends after that is bound by the
  sha256 it prints. The buffering dependency is removed rather than declared: the verifier reads the record, the
  printed output and the raw before it prints anything.
- **P2-7:** the draft check is "Code's mechanism for R2 3's order".
- **P2-8 (PARTLY):** the run's entry is to name the record's head and its `lock_draft.sha256`, and check the latter
  against the snapshot's sha256. No wrapper change.
- **P2-9:** if WRAPPER EXIT is not 0, Code stops, commits the raw and the record as they stand, and routes them to the
  seat. A re-run waits, with the record superseded under PIN-37.
- **P2-10:** a strike of a Q7 default needs a new G-PWR build, its review and pins, and a superseding run of record.
  This is in the draft's v1.0 §1 and in the entry.
- **P2-11:** the values-check raw splits the draft's later changes by which check saw them: pass 1, pass 2, and pass 3
  for this pass's changes.
- **P2-12:** the run's raw and the battery's keep their 2026-10-15 names whatever the run's date, so the reviewed
  wrapper runs unchanged. The header's stamp is `date -u`.
- **P2-13:** the filled entry is rewrapped to 120 columns, with each full sha256 on its own line.
- **Misses:**
  - **The dry runs' wording (I7 PARTLY):** "agree on every figure (r2 dropped r1's per-template launch lines; the
    READINGS texts were revised at r2 and r3)", in the entry and the raw's header.
  - **The post-commit check** compares the committed blobs (`git cat-file blob <commit>:<path>`) with the sha256 the
    wrapper prints. The verifier cannot be re-run after the commit. This is in the entry and disp_r14.
  - **P2-1's evidence corrections** are taken into the order statement: the planning map's own figure is 0.87843, and
    which figures were new is named.
  - **P2-6's and P2-5's anchors and P2-8's citation:** noted; no committed text is affected.

## The tools after this pass

- **Changes:** the wrapper's verifier call and header line; the verifier's interpreter line, its docstring and when
  it reads its inputs; `spot_v3.py`'s one line calling the verifier under `/usr/bin/python3 -I -S`. They are shown as
  their own diffs in the review raw.
- **The values-check raw's generator** (`gen_vc_raw.py`, no pre-pass copy kept) gained the third entry pass, and the
  three-way split of the draft's later changes by the pass that saw them.
- **The re-runs overwrote round 10's v3 trial outputs.** Round 10's versions were recovered from this check's
  pass-2 regeneration of the review raw, which carries both (disp_r14).
- **Re-runs on r13, in the review raw:**
  - `trial_wrapper.sh`: a lawful run passes, with wrapper exit 0; a committed `json_text` tamper fails on the package
    and the figures, with wrapper exit 1;
  - `spot_v3.py`: 11 cases, all as expected.

const SCHEMA = { type: 'object', properties: {
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, where: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['error', 'imprecise', 'note'] },
    finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'where', 'severity', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  verified: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['findings', 'verified'] }
const VER = { type: 'object', properties: {
  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
phase('Pass 3')
const rep = await agent(COMMON + `\n\nTASK (pass 3). Check EVERY added or changed line in the delta against its source: each quotation byte-exact (after the declared conventions), each attribution (the seat's, the PI's, Code's reading; is any departure stated as settled, or any reading stated as ruled), each fact in the preparation's order (against the files' birth dates and order), each claim about the tools (against their code and the re-runs), each sha256 and fill (recompute), the entry's lines under 120 columns. Check that disp_entry_vc2.md says what the delta does, no more and no less, and that each change answers the pass-2 finding it cites. Check that the departures are stated the same way in the entry, the draft and the review raw's header. Work dir ${S}/evc/p3.`, { label: 'evc:p3', phase: 'Pass 3', schema: SCHEMA })
phase('Refute 3')
const ref = await agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER of this values-check report (pass 3), verbatim:\n${JSON.stringify(rep)}\nFor EACH finding reproduce it and give CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with evidence and the exact fix; default to REFUTED if you cannot reproduce. Re-verify 3 'verified' items. Add to 'missed' anything wrong in the delta or disp_entry_vc2.md the report did not find. Work dir ${S}/evc/r3.`, { label: 'evc:r3', phase: 'Refute 3', schema: VER })
return { report: rep, refute: ref }

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 3 (disp_entry_vc3.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of the entry's values-check, pass 3 (pass 2's changes; one checker, with a strict refuter)

The refuter confirmed 12 of the 13 findings and P3-12 in part, re-verified three items (the fills, the delta, the tools
and trials), and added five misses. All are applied, in the refuter's form except:
- **P3-12(b):** applied in the checker's form, over the refuter's "no change";
- **P3-2:** pass 4 restored Code's reading "and the scratch runs are its review", dropped "at the time" in
  disp_entry_vc2's P2-2, and made the three statements one form;
- **P3-7:** disp_entry_vc2's lead is in Code's words, corrected at pass 4;
- **miss 1** (the ping's third promise) is kept by a wrapper change rather than by the run's entry, so the run's raw
  quotes the draft's line itself;
- **miss 2:** the overwrite is noted in disp_entry_vc2 at pass 4.

- **P3-1 (an error): the preparation's order.** The draft's §3 said the dry runs and the rounds came before "this
  draft named the analyzer". By the files' order only the first dry run did. The draft now has the raw's order: the
  first dry run before the draft's first text named the analyzer; the later dry runs and the rounds before the
  snapshot's commit. The entry and the raw's header say the same.
- **P3-2: one form for the departures in the entry, the draft and the raw's header.**
  - The synthetic-inputs departure is declared without a condition, with Code's reading of R2 3 beside it.
  - Q4's is "stated as a departure", with Code's reading beside it. Whether a statement in the raw's first committed
    text meets Q4's "at the time" is left to the seat.
  - disp_entry_vc2's "say the same" is corrected.
- **P3-3:** the entry counts three passes on itself, and says the raw shows the draft's later changes split by pass.
- **P3-4:** `spot_v3.py`'s one-line change is shown beside the tools' diff in the review raw. `gen_vc_raw.py`'s change
  is described in disp_entry_vc2, since no pre-pass copy was kept.
- **P3-5:** the .pth demonstration is placed where it ran, on a venv built from the same base interpreter.
- **P3-6:** "reads the record, the printed output and the raw before it prints". This is in disp_r14, disp_entry_vc2,
  the raw's section title and the verifier's comment. The comment change moves the verifier's sha256, so the trials
  and the replay were re-run.
- **P3-7:** disp_r14 quotes the refuter's fix in full. It says the interpreter half is applied, the pinned-copy half
  declined (the declared gap), and the re-verification half met in another form. disp_entry_vc2 names the findings
  applied in part.
- **P3-8:** the stub lines and the mid-sentence breaks are reflowed in the entry and the draft. Two over-long draft
  lines (v1.0 §0's Conventions, and v1.0 §1's "Two rounds", its first sub-bullet) were rewrapped, words unchanged;
  pass 4 reflowed what that left.
- **P3-9:** the raw's header adds that the 2026-10-13 map derived the projection's n_j and noted bars.py's .0976 on
  them, not of record.
- **P3-10:** the entry and the raw's header say the third wrapper and verifier were amended at this entry's
  values-check, which checked the amendment, not a review round. disp_r14 is excepted from "reads as of its round".
- **P3-11:**
  - "the sha256 the wrapper prints";
  - the run's entry is to quote the wrapper's last two terminal lines and check the committed blobs against them;
  - after a non-zero exit, the record and the raw are both superseded under PIN-37.
- **P3-12 (PARTLY):**
  - (a) one citation set in the draft and the entry: the hold "in the addendum's 'What proceeds meanwhile'", and "the
    answers' item 6; D1; v1.0 §5";
  - (b) "G-PWR re-runs and the draft's analyzer lines are redone (the hold)";
  - (c) "as Code's step-15 ping had proposed" for the first departure;
  - (d) the Q7 bullet is Code's reading "in this build", with the snapshot superseded with it.
- **P3-13:**
  - (a) disp_r14 limits the .pth claim to the record's figures, body and bound hashes. The interpreter-version field is
    the interpreter's own report;
  - (b) disp_entry_vc2 names the items the refuter re-verified.
- **Misses:**
  - **The ping's third promise** (G-PWR's raw quotes the draft's line naming the analyzer) is now kept. The wrapper's
    header quotes the committed draft's analyzer row, and the draft's §3 and the entry say so. This changes the
    wrapper's sha256, and the trials and the replay were re-run.
  - **Round 10's own v3 trial outputs,** overwritten at the re-runs, were recovered from pass 2's regenerated raw. The
    review raw carries them beside the re-runs; disp_r14 says so.
  - **The scratch assembly** `LOCK-m-F0.v1.0-draft.md` is re-assembled from the final amendment. It is a derived file,
    not committed.
  - "with its review and pins" in the entry.
  - **The .pth cause:** "with -S no site module runs, so no .pth reaches it", in disp_r14 and disp_entry_vc2.
- **The values-check raw's generator** (`gen_vc_raw.py`, no pre-pass copy kept) gained the fourth entry pass and the
  fourth split of the draft's later changes (pass 3's dispositions, which the entry's pass 4 checked).

const SCHEMA = { type: 'object', properties: {
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, where: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['error', 'imprecise', 'note'] },

  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' },

Work dir ${S}/evc/p4.`, { label: 'evc:p4', phase: 'Pass 4', schema: SCHEMA })
phase('Refute 4')
const ref = await agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER of this values-check report (pass 4), verbatim:\n${JSON.stringify(rep)}\nFor EACH finding reproduce it and give CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with evidence and the exact fix; default to REFUTED if you cannot reproduce. return { report: rep, refute: ref }

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 4 (disp_entry_vc4.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of the entry's values-check, pass 4 (pass 3's changes; one checker, with a strict refuter)

The refuter confirmed all eight findings and added eight misses. All are applied, in the refuter's form except where a
line says otherwise. The check stops here: each pass since the second found only refinements of the text the previous
pass's dispositions had added. Code checked this pass's changes mechanically: the quotations against their sources,
the fills recomputed, the widths, and the trials and replay re-run.

- **P4-1, the order:** round 1's lenses had begun on the inputs of record before the draft's first text named the
  analyzer. The texts say so. With the trials and replays re-run at this values-check (miss 8), they name everything
  that ran on those inputs before the snapshot's commit. This is in the draft's §3, the raw's header and the entry.
- **P4-2, one form for Q4's departure:**
  - the draft's §3 gains the seat clause ("whether that meets Q4's 'at the time' is the seat's to say");
  - the entry gains "and that the run of record follows its commit" and "the review raw's first committed text";
  - the entry's first departure gains ", which reads the draft's committed snapshot".
- **P4-3:** disp_entry_vc2's lead names its exceptions (P2-3, P2-4, P2-10, P2-13). P2-3's "(the refuter's form)" and
  P2-2's "at the time" are dropped, and the duplicate disp_r14 sentence is removed. P2-1 takes the refuter's sentence.
  The file now says it reads as of pass 2 except where a line says otherwise.
- **P4-4:** Code's reading "and the scratch runs are its review" is restored in all three texts, and disp_entry_vc2
  notes the overwritten outputs. disp_entry_vc3 lists its exceptions: P3-12(b) in the checker's form, miss 1 by a
  wrapper change, and P3-7's lead in Code's words.
- **P4-5:** the entry counts four passes. The generator's change is recorded in words in disp_entry_vc3.
- **P4-6:** disp_r14 and disp_entry_vc2 speak of the fix's three parts.
- **P4-7:** the draft's Conventions and §3 paragraphs and the raw's round-10 paragraph are reflowed. disp_entry_vc3's
  P3-8 records the two rewraps.
- **P4-8:** every diff in the review raw is labelled by file name, so no file times enter it. The spot_v3 section
  says its round-10 text was recovered from the pass-2 regeneration, and so does disp_r14.
- **Misses:**
  - the raw's header: disp_r12 and disp_r14 also record later rounds, and disp_r9, r11, r12 and r13 carry pass-1
    corrections;
  - "the run's raw's header (the wrapper's)", in the entry and the raw's header;
  - **the wrapper's launch check** now refuses unless the draft has exactly one analyzer row and it carries
    `scripts/m_analyze.py`'s sha256. The header quotes that row. The trials and the replay were re-run;
  - disp_entry_vc2's P2-1 takes the refuter's sentence;
  - the entry says the amendment's diff and re-runs are in round 10's section, and that the values-check, not a review
    round, checked it;
  - the draft's Q7 bullet gives the reason (the program row changes) and PIN-37, and the entry adds the snapshot's
    supersession;
  - miss 1's wrapper form is noted as an exception in disp_entry_vc3;
  - the trials and replays are named among the runs on the inputs of record.

====================================================================================================
fill_snapshot.py, verbatim
====================================================================================================

"""Fill the v1.0 draft's mechanical placeholders from the files as they will be committed, and write the snapshot G-PWR
reads. usage: python3 -I fill_snapshot.py <v10_amend.md> <out snapshot>; the files are read at their repository paths."""
import hashlib, json, re, sys
from pathlib import Path

R = Path("/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab")
amend, out = sys.argv[1:3]
sha = lambda p: hashlib.sha256((R / p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()  # noqa: E731
short = lambda h: f"{h[:8]}…{h[-4:]}"  # noqa: E731
vals = {"PROGRAM": sha("scripts/m_gpwr.py"),
        "PINIDX": sha("results/raw/2026-10-15_m-lock-v1.0_pin-index-addendum.txt"),
        "REANCHOR": sha("results/raw/2026-10-15_m-lock-v1.0_reanchoring.txt"),
        "REVIEW": sha("results/raw/2026-10-15_m-gpwr-program_review.txt")}
CLOSURE_FILES = ["valence_core/__init__.py", "valence_core/adapters/__init__.py", "valence_core/adapters/base.py",
                 "valence_core/e2d/__init__.py", "valence_core/e2d/audit_v3.py", "valence_core/e2d/config.py",
                 "valence_core/e2d/contentclass.py", "valence_core/e2d/contentclass_v4.py",
                 "valence_core/e2d/contentclass_v5.py", "valence_core/e2d/habit.py", "valence_core/session.py",
                 "valence_core/session_e2a.py", "valence_core/stats.py"]   # a lawful run's provenance.closure, keys
vals["CLOSURE"] = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps({rel: sha(rel) for rel in CLOSURE_FILES}, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
vals |= {"PROGRAM16": short(vals["PROGRAM"]), "PINIDX16": short(vals["PINIDX"])}
t = Path(amend).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
for k, v in vals.items():
    n = t.count(f"@@{k}@@")
    assert n == 1, (k, n)
    t = t.replace(f"@@{k}@@", v)
assert "@@" not in t, re.findall(r"@@\w+@@", t)
assert t.startswith("# LOCK-m-F0.md — v1.0 ")
assert "655e0f7f6743d0bf497f5ba97ce37f7febf9fcf6a45e068b5385ee945c5dc16f" in t
Path(out).write_text(t, encoding="utf-8")
for k, v in vals.items():
    print(f"{k} {v}")
print("snapshot", out, hashlib.sha256(t.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(), len(t.encode("utf-8")), "bytes")
